==============================================================================
Third-party attributions of the rvt-open engine
==============================================================================

The ACIS entity model and per-entity restore field-order schema in
crates/rvt-shapemanager/src/sab_graph/ are adapted (rewritten in Rust) from
ezdxf, Copyright (c) 2020, Manfred Moitzi, licensed under the MIT License
(MIT text below). https://github.com/mozman/ezdxf
The Revit-dialect record framing in this repository is rvt-open original
clean-room work and is not part of ezdxf.

crates/rvt-native-graph and crates/rvt-tessellate use earcut 0.4.9 from
https://github.com/ciscorn/earcut-rs, Copyright (c) 2016, Mapbox, and
Copyright (c) 2023, Taku Fukada. It is licensed under the ISC License
(ISC text below) and provides polygon triangulation with holes.

Additional Rust dependencies and their copyright and license notices are
listed in licenses/THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.txt. Compiler and runtime notices are
provided separately in the application distribution when those components
are included. Third-party notices do not assert that every listed build tool
or platform-specific component is executed by the converter.
